# Parity with RubyLLM 2.0, spec by spec

Generated by `bin/parity` from every `it` in `upstream/spec/ruby_llm/**/*_spec.rb` (308 files, 3747 examples, parsed with Prism by `bin/spec-examples`). Do not edit by hand: change `docs/parity-rules.txt` or cite the example from a test (`// spec: <file>:<line> <description>`), then rerun `bin/parity`.

| Status | Examples | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| PORTED | 2157 | a Rust test ports the example and cites it by file and line (or a rule names the test) |
| REPLAYED | 177 | a Rust test replays the example's own recorded cassette and asserts its request bodies |
| OWNED | 0 | not ported yet, and inside another in-flight agent lane (named) |
| N/A | 1413 | no Rust counterpart, with the reason (Ruby-only mechanism, or a provider this port leaves out) |
| MISSING | 0 | in scope for the port and not covered |
| **Total** | **3747** | |

N/A splits into 900 examples for providers this port leaves out (Azure, Bedrock, Vertex AI, Cohere, ElevenLabs, Deepgram, per BRIEF.md) and 513 with no Rust counterpart.

| Line | Example | Status | Evidence / reason |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 20 | records zero tokens for attempts that never reached the provider | PORTED | src/chat.rs failure_tokens (ConnectionFailed = zero), tests/review_regressions.rs |
| 31 | retains absent model identity and unknown token accounting for model-free operations | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 49 | records zero tokens for attempts the provider refused | PORTED | tests/review_regressions.rs::a_retried_429_keeps_the_cost_known_and_links_to_the_answer |
| 61 | prices a call whose first attempt was rate limited | PORTED | tests/review_regressions.rs::a_retried_429_keeps_the_cost_known_and_links_to_the_answer |
| 80 | prices against the requested model when the provider echoes an unregistered id | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 97 | keeps tokens unknown for attempts that may have been billed | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 129 | provider-specific message pricing > replaces a providerless lookup before recording the cost | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 142 | provider-specific message pricing > falls back to the requested model when only another provider knows the echoed id | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 156 | provider-specific message pricing > uses a different echoed model when it belongs to the same provider | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 171 | provider-specific message pricing > preserves a provider-reported cost of #{amount} | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 185 | provider-specific message pricing > preserves an explicitly supplied cost | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 197 | provider-specific message pricing > leaves missing provider pricing unknown | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 213 | recognizes an exact cost even when token counts are unavailable | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 230 | keeps aggregate cost unknown when any potentially billed attempt is unknown | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 249 | keeps stream tokens observed before a never-sent classification | PORTED | tests/review_regressions.rs::an_error_after_a_delivered_chunk_is_not_retried (observed tokens billed) |
| 259 | refuses an unknown operation or status | N/A | Rust type system: operation and status are enums |
| 269 | summarizes an entry for inspection | N/A | Ruby-only: inspect summary |
| 278 | records a cancelled attempt as cancelled | PORTED | tests/spec_chat_loop.rs::cancel_from_the_stream_block_stops_the_stream (Cancelled entry path in chat.rs) |
| 287 | ignores a second failure for an attempt that already finished | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 296 | fails every attempt still in flight | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
| 306 | ignores a chunk when nothing is in flight | PORTED | `crates/rust_llm/tests/spec_usage.rs` |
